  var RETURN_URL = '%2Fresult';
  var SITE_KEY = '0x4AAAAAAC_EnAN-B9mMG11l';
  function onTurnstileSuccess(token) {
    fetch('/Challenge', {
      method:  'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body:    JSON.stringify({ token: token, returnUrl: decodeURIComponent(RETURN_URL) })
    })
    .then(function(r) {
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error('failed');

    .catch(function() {

      turnstile.reset('#widget');
    });
  }
  function onLoad() {
    turnstile.render('#widget', {
      sitekey:  SITE_KEY,
      theme:    'light',
      callback: onTurnstileSuccess
    });
  }
</script>

    .then(function(r) {
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error('failed');
      return r.json();
    })
    .then(function(data) {
      window.location.href = data.redirect;
    })
    .catch(function() {
      document.getElementById('err').style.display = 'block';
      turnstile.reset('#widget');
    });
  }
  function onLoad() {
    turnstile.render('#widget', {
      sitekey:  SITE_KEY,
      theme:    'light',
      callback: onTurnstileSuccess
    });
  }
</script>

  function onTurnstileSuccess(token) {
    fetch('/Challenge', {
      method:  'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},

  var RETURN_URL = '%2Fresult';
  var SITE_KEY = '0x4AAAAAAC_EnAN-B9mMG11l';
  function onTurnstileSuccess(token) {
    fetch('/Challenge', {
      method:  'POST',
      headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body:    JSON.stringify({ token: token, returnUrl: decodeURIComponent(RETURN_URL) })
    })
    .then(function(r) {
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error('failed');
      return r.json();
    })
    .then(function(data) {
      window.location.href = data.redirect;
    })
    .catch(function() {
      document.getElementById('err').style.display = 'block';
      turnstile.reset('#widget');
    });
  }
  function onLoad() {
    turnstile.render('#widget', {
      sitekey:  SITE_KEY,
      theme:    'light',
      callback: onTurnstileSuccess
    });
  }

      turnstile.reset('#widget');
    });
  }
  function onLoad() {
    turnstile.render('#widget', {
      sitekey:  SITE_KEY,
      theme:    'light',
      callback: onTurnstileSuccess
    });
  }
</script>
